nan_00060.jpg,"Thousands of flamingos turn Sambhar Lake pink during their annual migration. India s largest inland salt lake becomes a seasonal home for huge flocks of migratory flamingos, which arrive between October and March. Their bright pink feathers, reflected across the shallow waters of the lake, create an incredible sight, and wildlife photographer captured it beautifully."
nan_00061.jpg,"Sainte-Chapelle is one of the finest examples of Rayonnant Gothic architecture, built by King Louis IX between 1242 and 1248. The chapel has two levels, but the upper chapel is what makes it so special. Its walls are almost entirely covered by 15 enormous stained-glass windows made up of more than 1,100 panels. The glass tells biblical stories in deep blues, reds, and golds, and when sunlight comes through, the whole space seems to glow. By: Daniel Vel squez"

nan_v00504_f000.jpg,"Once a year, Nara s Great Buddha gets a very serious spring cleaning. Every August 7, Todaiji Temple holds Ominugui, a traditional ceremony dedicated to removing a year s worth of dust and preparing the statue for Obon. Around 120 monks and helpers take part, using long brooms and dusters to clean the enormous Buddha, which stands roughly 15 meters tall. Some are even raised by gondola to reach the higher sections."
